## What the recorded description got wrong

Measured at planning against the pin; carried here so the lane and the
unifier read the corrected facts, not the brief's or the ledger's:

- **The `help-ensure` "fixture rebuild" rests on a false premise.** The
  WCAG survey (`:305`, `:453`) says `help_doc_ensure_equivalence` "compares
  the vendored `help/` tree through a committed fixture" and that this lane
  "rebuilds the help-ensure fixture" after re-vendoring four pages. The
  family does NOT read `<repo>/help/`: both sides walk the committed
  TWO-page tree `harness/oracle/fixtures/help-ensure/help/{aurora.md,
  brahma-console.md}` (`help_doc_ensure_equivalence.rs:7-9`, `:209` —
  `load_help_source_files(&tree_root)` over the fixture dir; the builder
  `build-help-ensure-fixture.ts:16` "share ONE tree"). `aurora.md` exists in
  NO v4 tree (`git show 322a5488e:help/aurora.md` → "does not exist"), and
  the fixture's `brahma-console.md` (md5 `c1afbf93a40ad5213229923a172c907d`)
  is a deliberately frozen vintage — equal to NEITHER the baseline's
  (`8bffd35d4476df39a90273b4c308d96c`) NOR the pin's
  (`cf7c33d67ec8d7e1c191f1c0bca8078d`); twelve `help-ensure.json` scenarios
  key their hashes to it. So the re-vendor moves nothing in that family and
  "rebuilding" the committed tree would re-mint twelve scenarios for no
  behaviour. **The binding text (§S.5 (21), the Ownership row: "the
  `help-ensure` fixture rebuilt (`help_doc_ensure_equivalence.rs:55-62`)")
  is read as what `:47-62` IS: the per-run REGEN** — the builder writes
  `qt-ensure-<scenario>-main.db` ×12 into `$QT_FIXTURE_ENSURE_DIR` and the
  jest oracle runs over them; both are rebuilt from the pin and the family
  run GREEN (it SKIPped on P4.D339's branch — the record says so). The
  committed tree and JSON are NOT edited (R-3). If the human or the unifier
  wants the two-page tree moved to the pin's `brahma-console.md`, that is a
  separate, named decision (twelve hash re-mints) — asked in the lane record,
  not done.
- **The theme split is OVERRULED.** The vendor survey §D.3 gave this lane
  the twelve `theme.json` / `tokens.json` byte copies and §6 / §8 / §11
  built on it (the `tokens.json` user-visibility note, a SPA bump, a theme
  dogfood row). Round ruling R-M (lane assignments; §R.11 R-M; §R.10): **ALL
  18 `apps/web/public/themes/*` files are P4.D357's** — this lane copies
  NONE, bumps NOTHING, and §R.6's "The SPA: … P4.D360 (the theme byte
  copies)" line is superseded by R-M (record it; the unifier's §S.3 recount
  excludes this lane).
- **`cb94e6e99`'s harness drop is at `orchestrator_tier3_equivalence.rs:1438`**
  (header `:16-17`, doc `:1397`), not the ledger's `:1325` (P4.D348's
  measurement). The ratification cites `:1438`.
- **The ledger's `b09ff5c86` row says "six mirrored paths deleted / moved"**;
  measured: FIVE source paths vanish or move (3 `D` + 2 `R`), ONE page is
  born (`composer-document-references.md`), SIX pages are edited

  baseline `322a5488e` is behind the target; the live checkout is past it

  - **The pin.** Every lane regenerates from a LANE-UNIQUE detached pin:

    /tmp/qt-v4-pin-<orderid-lowercase>-236c04e35 236c04e35`. Add the THREE

    7789ebed6` is NOT a regen pin — never run an oracle from it.)

    every pin path from a quoted literal; check a fresh NDJSON is non-empty

  - **The sweep driver** is the sanctioned per-family path: `cd <the lane's
    worktree> && /opt/homebrew/bin/python3.13 harness/tools/recipe_sweep.py
    --run <family> --v4 "$PIN" --v5w <the lane's ABSOLUTE SPACELESS worktree
    path>`. **`--v5w` is the tree the regen WRITES INTO**, so it is the
    lane's own worktree (`~/source/quilltap-v5/.claude/worktrees/<orderid-
    lowercase>` — spaceless; memory note `sweep-driver-spaced-path`), NEVER
    `main`'s tree. From a `.claude/` venue add `--force`; `--families` wants
    the FULL test-file stem, comma-separated. **Twenty lanes share ONE
    machine: never two sweeps at once; a lane announces a sweep to the
    human before starting it.** A family reporting `SKIP` in 0.00 s did not
    run. The system python 3.9 cannot parse the driver. The driver does
    NOT rewrite a recipe's `/tmp` outputs (memory note
    `parallel-lane-shared-resources` §3) — copy outputs to a lane-private
    dir right after a sweep.

  - (h) **The keystone-memory facts the row overstated:** `keystone` is a
    FLAG, not a tier (R-E) — `bucketCandidates` skips `memory.keystone`
    rows AFTER `rowsLoaded++`, BEFORE the `CONSOLIDATED` branch; `isDemotable
    = hot && AUTO && !keystone`; `policyProtectionOf` answers `'keystone'`
    FIRST and `sparedBy`'s key order becomes `{keystone, manual, digest,
    cold}`; a keystone fold survivor takes NO footnotes and an EMPTY patch
    but keeps links + the reinforcement delta; `cascadeEmptiedDigests` keeps
    a keystone digest whose last member retires (DEBUG `Keystone digest kept
    though its last member retires`) and its `added` now counts only rows
    actually doomed (`if (!grew) break`) — port as shipped; `protection()`
    answers `{score: 1, protected: true, protectedBySource: true}`. In
    recall, "every digest is cut before any keystone" holds only INSIDE the
    inner circle (`innerCircleTrimOrder` sorts `Number(b.keystone) −
    Number(a.keystone)` first): present-character keystones are LIFTED into
    the circle at `tier −1` ahead of that character's digests, self
    keystones join the anchors, and `renderArchive` / `renderHead` render a
    keystone's `content` first (`summary` first otherwise). `KEYSTONES_PER_
    SUBJECT_MAX = 5`; the v4 quirk — `POST /memories` with `keystone: true`
    answers 400 `The memory was saved, but not as a keystone: <msg>` AFTER
    writing the row — is ported as the bytes (Q3). `keystone` appears in
    EVERY parsed Memory (between `consolidatedAt` and `createdAt`), so every
    byte pin of a memory row moves; `hydrateRawRows` coerces number / bigint
    / null to a boolean and DROPS a string cell with the WARN.
  - (i) **The WCAG ownership rule** (wcag survey §7.3): "the lane with the
    larger or structural hunk owns the file; the a11y lane records a HANDOFF
    naming the rule(s) R1–R12 and v4's bytes; the owner applies them in its
    own commit; the unifier checks the handoff list. Ties go to the feature
    lane (the a11y edits are mechanical)." Every sibling SPA lane applies
    rules **R1–R12** (the binding text is Shared contract C9) to the files it
    owns and records each as a `HANDOFF:` for P4.D358's census; P4.D358's
    static census script takes an allowlist for sibling-owned files, which
    the unifier empties. Already CONVERGED (not reds): the sidebar switches,
    `Allow Any Character`, the edit box, the composer `Message`, the
    slide-over's focus. The real gap: of 34 overlay components, 13 have no
    `role="dialog"`; `qt-modal` (77 hosts) carries `role` + `aria-modal` +
    the close label but NO `aria-labelledby` / `tabindex` / focus / Escape.

- **§R.5 — Log lines.** Every line this round ports or moves lands with a
  capture pin (the thread-scoped rig) AND its silence leg.
  - A line emitted INSIDE a `Db::write` closure runs on the writer thread and
    is INVISIBLE to the rig: run the home on the caller thread (memory note
    `capture-rig-writer-thread`) or use `global_capture`.
  - A `db::fallback` line renders the BARE SQLite / Zod message
    (`error_text`), never `sqlite error: …`.
  - A structured v4 field that is an array or object renders through the
    `…Json` file-layer convention; v4's camelCase keys are the keys; an
    `undefined` v4 field is OMITTED; child-logger bindings are fields on the
    line; `durationMs` and other wall-clock fields are MASKED.
  - Open before `captured_with`; a v4 catch UNREACHABLE through v4's real
    code is pinned NEGATIVE with a v5 fallback home.
  - **Migration lines follow the standing precedent** (P4.D249 / P4.D304 /
    P4.D315 / P4.D324, `host.rs`): v4's migration INFO / ERROR texts are
    NO-PORT and each boot ensure logs ONE v5-worded INFO per added column /
    table / index.
  - **Tag ownership:** **P4.D341** — the five ensures' boot INFOs (incl.
    `Fitted the custom-tool intervention toggle`), `Refusing memory edit:
    keystone rule`, `[Memories API] Keystone mark refused after manual
    create`, `Keystone digest kept though its last member retires`, `Error
    finding keystones` / `Error counting keystones` (the repository-fallback
    class), `Memory failed validation in keystone fetch`, the seeder's
    `Built-in roleplay template seeded` / `Roleplay template updated
    successfully`. **P4.D342** — `[InnerCircle] Resolved inner circle`,
    `[FrozenArchive] Composed archive` (its keys change ONCE, both commits',
    one lane) and the cache-miss `[FrozenArchive] Computed archive`.
    **P4.D343** — `Effect ceiling applied to tool list`, `Autonomous room:
    destructive tools withheld by effect ceiling`, `Tool call refused: above
    the turn's effect ceiling` / `Tool call within the turn's effect
    ceiling`, `Triggered custom tool skipped — above the turn's effect

    round`, `StorytellerBidCall`, the repository's `Storyteller bid dropped —

- **§R.7 — Red-first.**
  - **Measure red before touching source** — on unported `main` for the
    wave-0 lanes, on the `KEYSTONE` tip for its dependents (a contract
    refusal arm answering `not_available` IS the red). The lane record
    carries the red count per family. A test that passes before the fix is
    a test of nothing.
  - **Ruled divergences** are NAMED both-ways tables in the idiom of
    `FRESH_STORE_RESIDUAL` / `BOOT_CREATED_HISTORY_TABLE`, so a v4
    convergence trips them. This round's planned names: **P4.D345**
    `HOLD_KEEP_ALIVE_NO_STREAM` (v4's 15 s keep-alive ping — no v5
    counterpart by architecture), `ROUND_DROPS_MIN_EXCLUSIVE` (only if the
    corpus shows it); **P4.D354** `REFUSING_SEAM_LEAVES_PDF_UNEXTRACTED`
    (the backfill and the `_indexed` bridges leave a PDF / DOCX row at
    `'none'` while `DocumentTextExtractor::is_available()` is `false`; the
    REST path keeps v4's `failed` bytes); **P4.D347** `ECHO_SLICE_SURROGATE`
    + `SENTENCE_SLICE_SURROGATE` (a JS `slice` that cuts a surrogate pair —
    v5 rounds to the code-point boundary); **P4.D343** the dry scope's
    table-delta proof (whatever the `DryBuildContextSeams` wrapper does not
    cover is a recorded divergence, never a silent write) and `plugin_tool_
    effects` as NO-COUNTERPART; **P4.D350** `PUBLISH_AFTER_COMMIT` only if
    the human overrules its R-1 (v4 publishes at the write; v5 after the
    commit); **P4.D355** the explicit `terminal_sessions` DELETE on chat
    delete (v5's cascade stand-in on a generateDDL table with no FK — named
    in its record; R-R); **P4.D341** none owed (Q3's post-write 400 is
    ported as bytes); **P4.D352** an ADAPTER divergence recorded in the SPA
    (ProseMirror `Decoration.inline` for v4's CSS Custom Highlight API; the
    four `::highlight(name)` rules become class rules with the same tokens);
    **P4.D353** a Tier-3 code-block note only if R-B is overruled to (C).

- **§R.8 — The unifier's accumulation.** Each lane's record states its
  `Cargo.toml` versions at close; the unifier takes the HIGHEST and bumps +1
  for the union commit; `Cargo.lock` follows. The SPA: the highest of the
  SPA lanes, +1. CHANGELOG entries and `status-log.md` lane records
  union-merge (append-only).
- **§R.9 — One machine.**
  - **Workspace gates:** at most TWO Rust workspace gates at once. Count them
    with ONE `ps -ax -o pid,etime,command | grep "cargo test --workspace" |
    grep -v grep`; if two run, queue yours in ONE background command waiting
    on the siblings' PIDs (`while kill -0 <pidA> && kill -0 <pidB>; do sleep
    20; done; sleep $((RANDOM % 20)); <re-count once; if still two, wait on
    those PIDs>; cargo test …`), never on a pattern (memory notes
    `no-pattern-kill-on-shared-machine`, `parallel-lane-shared-resources`
    §2 — co-waiters all fire on one exit; re-count after the wait).
  - **Sweeps and Playwright:** one sweep at a time (announced); one
    Playwright run at a time (lanes by file only; the full suite is the
    unifier's). New own-instance e2e files' server ports are unique per
    lane (4319–4340, 4343–4350, 4352 and mocks 45301–45304, 45340–45348 are
    taken): **P4.D344 4353 / mock 45351**, **P4.D346 4354 / mock 45352**,
    **P4.D352 4355 / mock 45353**, **P4.D353 4356 / mock 45354**, **P4.D358
    4357 / mock 45355**, **P4.D359 4358 / mock 45356**, **P4.D355 4359 /
    mock 45357** (only if a terminal e2e is added). A lane that only edits
    an EXISTING spec takes no port.
  - **Long jobs:** `run_in_background: true`, full log in a lane-private
    file, awaited by the completion notification — NEVER a `pgrep`/`grep`
    poll loop (CLAUDE.md), never `tail -N` on a gate log. A gate reads its
    oracles and source at RUN time: no regen and no source edit while one
    runs (memory note `lane-gate-hygiene`).
  - **Targets:** clone main's `target/` before the first build (`cp -cR`;
    `/carryout` rule 8 — same volume only, memory note
    `apfs-clone-same-volume-only`); main's `target/` was warmed by the
    `/setupphase` chain on 2026-10-29 at the orders' commit (the test /
    clippy set the lanes clone; the release bins were swept — an
    own-instance e2e that prefers `target/release/*` must delete the stale
    release bins from its clone or build release, memory note
    `lane-spa-env-node-modules-and-release-bins`); a stacked lane may clone
    its parent lane's worktree target instead, when that lane is not
    mid-build.
  - **`apps/web/dist/`:** a fresh worktree has none — build it before any
    workspace clippy / test. **`apps/web/node_modules`:** `npm ci` in the
    lane (a cloned copy fails every vitest spec with NG0203).
  - **Concurrency:** the cap is the two-gate rule, not the lane count;
    twenty lanes do NOT all run at once (§S.0 waves). Disk at planning:
    144 GiB free on the repo volume with `target/` at 347 GB BEFORE the
    warm sweep — the human sizes the waves from the post-sweep numbers in
    the hand-off report.

- **§R.11 — Rulings for the human.** Each order's "Rulings made at
  planning" are the planner's calls; the human may overrule any before lane
  start (an overruled ruling is a STOP item for that lane). The round-level
  ones to read FIRST (none is yet approved — the human approves or
  overrules at the orders' hand-off):
  - ⚖ **THE NPM CACHE GAP (vendor survey §F.4 — a PRE-ROUND BLOCKER):** the
    human's live checkout is at `4.12.0-dev.5` (`openai` 7.45.1, the v13
    driver), so the npm cache never saw the pin's versions — 44 of the 188
    tarballs the pin's lock adds / moves are `ENOTCACHED`; `npm ci --offline`
    at the pin FAILS as written. **The planner's call: ONE online `npm ci
    --no-audit --no-fund` in a pin worktree before any lane starts** (the
    human, or the first lane with the human's go-ahead), which populates the
    cache for every lane's `--offline` rebuild; record the resulting
    `node_modules/.package-lock.json` stamp. The alternative (the live

- **§R.14 — Stacking rules.** Each named stacking commit — `KEYSTONE`
  (P4.D341), `CONTEXT` (P4.D347), `SPINE` (P4.D348), `CATALOG` (P4.D350's
  FIRST commit), `FILES` (P4.D354) — is ONE commit whose subject starts with
  its name; the owner records the sha in its status header ON ITS LANE
  BRANCH the moment it lands. A dependent lane — P4.D347 / P4.D342 / P4.D351
  / P4.D356 from `KEYSTONE`; P4.D348 from `CONTEXT`; P4.D343 / P4.D345 from
  `SPINE` (which is on `CONTEXT` on `KEYSTONE`); P4.D349 from `CATALOG`;
  P4.D355 from `FILES` — finds the sha per `/carryout` rule 2 (`git log --all
  --format='%h %s' | grep -E '^[0-9a-f]+ <NAME>:'`, confirmed against the
  parent's lane-branch copy of the order) and branches from it — never from
  `main`, never a guessed sha; if the parent has not recorded it, STOP and
  report. A stacked lane may PRE-START from `main` on work that calls none
  of its parent's contract names — P4.D342's prompt bytes (the four
  extraction ladders + the consolidation tail) + the inner-circle resolver
  (`services/inner_circle.rs`, the tuning knobs); P4.D343's `tools/effects.rs`
  + `pascal/tool_effect.rs` + `StreamParams.tool_choice` + the `tool-build`
  re-aim (its unit E) + the pure `storyteller/*` modules; P4.D345's hold
  registry + eligibility / overrides / recompute / spans + the engine's
  `ReplayInputs` + the ONE corpus regen + the schema re-vendor; P4.D351's
  `services/annotations.rs` + `template_prompt_hint.rs` ports; P4.D356's
  NanoGPT half + the Tier R verify; P4.D349's `pronoun-presets` /
  `pronoun-guidance` ports + the image-prompt `humanoid` threading; P4.D355's
  `db/log_entries.rs` + the terminal host; P4.D348's `operator_post.rs` /
  `whisper_send.rs` / `turn_control.rs` additions and the funnel recorder;
  P4.D347 has no pre-start (its first unit widens `RawMessage`, which rides
  the keystone's row shapes) — rebasing onto the parent's commit before the
  first commit that does call a contract name; that rebase is the one
  sanctioned exception to "no rebase mid-lane". Two sibling-stacked parts
  (not lane-named commits): P4.D346's grammar-twin part (`pascal/custom-tool-
  types.ts` + the corpus copy + the spec header) stacks on P4.D345's Tier-1
  commit, or authors blind and lets the unifier run `custom-tool-types.
  corpus.spec.ts` — the order states which; P4.D359's chips stack on
  P4.D353's Tier-1 commit (the `message-content.ts` hook). Every other lane
  cuts from `main` at the orders' commit.

## §S — Unifier wires (what no single lane can do)

- **§S.0 — Waves (the recommended execution; the two-gate cap rules the
  machine, not the lane count).**
  - **Wave 0, from the orders' commit, at once:** P4.D341 (→ `KEYSTONE`,
    the Opus / Fable tier), P4.D350 (→ `CATALOG` as its FIRST commit, then
    its own units), P4.D354 (→ `FILES`), and in parallel P4.D360, P4.D357,
    P4.D358. That is six worktrees.
  - **Wave 1, as slots free (still from the orders' commit):** the five SPA
    feature lanes P4.D344, P4.D346, P4.D352, P4.D353, P4.D359 (each authors
    against the contracts with gated beats; P4.D346's grammar-twin part and
    P4.D359's chips wait for their sibling's Tier-1 commit).
  - **Wave A, from `KEYSTONE`:** P4.D347 (→ `CONTEXT`) FIRST, then P4.D342,
    P4.D351, P4.D356 (its NanoGPT + Tier R half may have PRE-STARTED from
    `main`). **From `CATALOG`:** P4.D349 — FIRST among the independents
    (R-H). **From `FILES`:** P4.D355.
  - **Wave B, from `CONTEXT`:** P4.D348 (→ `SPINE`).
  - **Wave C, from `SPINE`:** P4.D343 and P4.D345 (the two turn-side
    consumers every later union depends on).

  Within a wave every lane compiles against the C sections below it alone.
- **§S.1 — The pick order.** P4.D341 → P4.D350 → P4.D349 → P4.D347 →
  P4.D348 → P4.D343 → P4.D345 → P4.D342 → P4.D351 → P4.D356 → P4.D354 →
  P4.D355 → P4.D357 → P4.D344 → P4.D346 → P4.D352 → P4.D353 → P4.D359 →
  P4.D360 → P4.D358. The reasons per adjacency: `KEYSTONE` first (every
  server lane's row shapes, the dumps, the ensures); P4.D350 next because
  `CATALOG` is the base P4.D349 branches from, and P4.D349 right after it
  (R-H — the three LIVE-DATA erasure writers land before anything else
  touches a vault); P4.D347 → P4.D348 → P4.D343 → P4.D345 in their stacking
  order (`CONTEXT` on `KEYSTONE`, `SPINE` on `CONTEXT`, the two consumers of
  S1 / S2 / C1 / `TurnStopReach` on `SPINE`; P4.D343 before P4.D345 so
  `trigger_runner.rs`'s pre-run ceiling block precedes the post-run `Vetoed`
  arm and `realtime/types.rs` lands `storytellerRounds` NINTH before
  `customToolInterventions` TENTH); P4.D342 and P4.D351 (keystone-only
  consumers, no spine seam) after the spine chain; P4.D356 after P4.D345 so
  the `customToolIntervention` arm it writes for P4.D345 and the
  `punctuationWarnings` key it writes for P4.D352 are in the tree when their
  families re-run, and after P4.D351 so `settings-routes` is regenerated
  ONCE at the end of the server picks; P4.D354 → P4.D355 (`FILES` then the
  two bridge sites switched); P4.D357 before every SPA lane (the CSS lines
  the fenced appends sit beside); P4.D344 before P4.D346 (`_index.css`
  imports in v4's order; `RealtimeTopicAppend`'s SPA half NINTH then TENTH;
  `LibraryEntryKeys`' SPA badges in v4's order); P4.D352 before P4.D353
  (`renderMarkdownToHtml` is P4.D352's; P4.D353 post-passes its HTML);
  P4.D353 before P4.D359 (P4.D359's chips stack on P4.D353's `message-
  content.ts` hook; its `_variables.css` append sits after P4.D353's);
  P4.D360 LATE (its help / docs guards judge a union carrying every server
  feature; its schema-guard CHECK sees the final constants; the P4.D339
  precedent: fourteenth) — nineteenth here, before P4.D358; P4.D358 LAST
  (its census allowlist for sibling-owned HANDOFF files is emptied against
  the finished union; its toast-selector first commit is cherry-picked
  EARLIER by any sibling that edits one of the 20 spec files).

  "Delete all my data" emptying `log_entries`, the backup still without
  `terminal_sessions`); **a terminal kill that lands** (bash exits on
  SIGHUP; a `sleep 9999` shell → the SIGKILL WARN after 3 s; deleting a chat
  with a live Ariel → `[PTY] Terminated sessions of deleted chat` + the rows
  gone on a v5-provisioned instance; the daily `Swept orphaned terminal
  transcripts` after the 1 h grace); **the files round** (💸 `doc_read_file`
  / `doc_grep` / `doc_list_files` with `**/CHANGELOG.md` and `*.{md,txt}` on
  attached uploads; the first-boot backfill INFOs and `background_jobs`
  rows — 💸 if the embedding profile is remote; the Docker folder picker at
  `/app/quilltap`; `quilltap sync --dry-run` refusal in the container;
  `docker exec quilltap quilltap db --lock-status`; a corrupt sibling → a
  MOUNT_CHUNK job RETRIES; 💸 the wizard's PDF import still logs the new
  WARN text); **the settings smalls** (`PUT /settings/chat {"coreWhisper":
  {"enabled":false}}` both transports; `{"foo":1}` → `Invalid settings:
  unknown key foo`; a planted `cheapLLMSettings = 5` → ONE row, the
  existing id with defaults + the WARN, the save writing the repair back;
  `quilltap db --count chats` under `FORCE_COLOR=1` a bare number); 💸
  **NanoGPT image sizes fitted** (a portrait avatar on a tier model posts
  `size: 1k, aspect_ratio: 2:3`; a non-square turned down → the square retry
  WARN and a square picture — the human's authorization, the P4.D283 C5
  precedent); **the WCAG pass** (six packs × light / dark, the toasts, the
  queue badges; reduced-motion; VoiceOver: `Skip to content`, the Salon
  `<h1>`, 💸 one real turn announced `<name> replied: …`, five dialogs'
  title + role + Escape + focus return; keyboard-only F6 out of Ariel,
  Enter / Space on the clickable rows; 320 px toolbar wrap and 24 px tab
  closes; 💸 the floating Brahma console over a modal; the `qt-checkbox`
  Automation row under Madman's Box); **the vendor set** (the Guide lists 137
  pages with `Diagrams` and `Storyteller Mode` in place; `help_docs` 135 →
  137 rows on first boot with 35 `updatedAt` moves — 💸 the re-embed only if
  a profile is configured; the six theme previews showing the pin's tokens).
- **§S.9 — The gated beats flip LIVE.** P4.D344 / P4.D346 / P4.D352 /
  P4.D353 / P4.D359 author their Playwright beats behind gate constants
  (`const P4D343_SERVER_LANDED = false` etc.), with throwaway stand-ins
  deleted at the flip. On the union the unifier flips each to `true`,
  deletes the stand-ins, runs each touched spec by file (alone; 4353–4359
  free), then the full suite. A red is root-caused against the server logs
  before it is accepted as the recorded timing class.

- **C2 — the Storyteller wire W1 (P4.D343 builds, P4.D344 consumes;
  `crates/quilltap-core/src/api/types.rs` + `apps/web/src/app/core/core-
  contract.ts`):** `Request::ChatUpdate { …, turn_mode: Option<Option<Value>>
  }` (`double_option`, serde key `turnMode`, a sibling of `chat`; absent →
  untouched; `"rotation"` / `"storyteller"` → `set_turn_mode` (+ `abandon_
  storyteller_round(chat_id, "abandoned")` on leaving); `null` or anything
  else → `BadRequest "Validation error"`, nothing written) ↔ `ChatUpdate
  Request.turnMode?: 'rotation' | 'storyteller'`; the bag key `chat.
  storytellerInviteFilter: boolean` (non-boolean → the whole PUT refused).
  `Request::ChatStorytellerRound { chat_id }` → `Response::ChatStoryteller
  Round(Value)` = `{ "round": StorytellerRound | null }`. `Request::Chat
  StorytellerRun { chat_id, round_id: String, participant_ids: Vec<String>,
  order: Option<String>, keep_order: Option<bool> }` (keys `roundId`,
  `participantIds`, `order` default `"sequential"`, `keepOrder`; a non-enum
  `order` → `BadRequest "Invalid request"`) → an EMPTY reply (`Response::Ok`
  / `Null` — the web edge answers the SSE stream; `{ ended }` would be extra
  information v4 never answers); refusals `Conflict` 409 `That round is no
  longer open`, `NotFound` 404 `That seat is not on this round` / `Chat not
  found`, `BadRequest` 400 `Choose at least one bid, or let the round lie` /
  `Only a seat that bid to speak can be chosen` / `A Destroy bid must be
  chosen on its own`. `Request::ChatStorytellerAsk { chat_id, round_id,
  participant_id }` → `Response::ChatStorytellerAsk(Value)` = `{ "bid":
  StorytellerBid }` (the ANSWERED card). `Request::ChatStorytellerLie {
  chat_id, round_id }` → `{ "closed": true }`. Frames on `EventPayload::Chat
  (ChatEvent)`: `{"storytellerRound": StorytellerRound}`, `{"storytellerBid":
  {"roundId", "bid": StorytellerBid}}`, `{"roundOpen": true, "roundId"}`,
  `chainComplete` with `reason: "round_open"` (the literal; no enum change);
  a concurrent turn's frames carry `"participantId"` (when the frame lacked
  one) and `"concurrent": true` as the LAST two keys (`ChatEvent::Tagged`
  flattening inner then the two keys). The chat GET wire: `turnMode:
  'rotation' | 'storyteller'` (never null on the wire), `storytellerInvite
  Filter: boolean`, both after `isPaused`. Realtime topic `"storytellerRounds"`
  scoped by chat id → `chatKeys.storytellerRound(chatId) = ['chat', chatId,
  'storytellerRound']`. `LlmLogDto.storytellerRoundId?: string | null`; log
  type `"STORYTELLER_BID"`. `CustomToolLibraryEntry.effect: 'read' | 'write'`
  after `intervention` (T2). TS types `StorytellerBid` (key order
  `participantId, characterId, characterName, status, statedTier?, tier?,
  tierRaised, tools, plan?, reason?, talkativeness, silent, askedAt?,
  answeredAt?`), `StorytellerRound` (`id, chatId, triggerMessageId, status,
  bids, selection, closedReason, createdAt, updatedAt, closedAt`),
  `StorytellerSelection { participantIds, order: Order | null, chosenAt,
  names }`, `StorytellerBidRecord { roundId, tier, plan, toolsNamed }`,
  `StorytellerTier` (`simple | lookup | act | destroy`), `StorytellerOrder`
  (`sequential | simultaneous`), `TIER_LABELS` — field for field, optional /
  nullable preserved. P4.D344 codes to this with `it.skipIf(SERVER_LANDED)`
  gates.
- **C3 — the intervention wire `InterventionWire` (P4.D345 builds, P4.D346
  consumes; the BINDING copy is P4.D345's order):** dispatch verbs
  `{"type":"chatCustomToolInterventionsList","chatId"}` → `Response::
  CustomToolInterventions(Value)` = `{"holds": InterventionHoldView[]}`;
  `{"type":"chatCustomToolInterventionPreview","chatId","id","overrides"}` →
  `Response::CustomToolInterventionPreview(Value)` = `{"preview":
  InterventionPreview}`; `{"type":"chatCustomToolInterventionReask","chatId",
  "id","overrides"}` → `Response::CustomToolInterventionReask(Value)` =
  `{"preview", "consults": [{ok, output, prompt}]}`; `{"type":"chatCustom
  ToolInterventionResolve","chatId","id","decision"}` → `Response::CustomTool
  InterventionResolve(Value)` = `{"resolved": "deal" | "as-rolled" | "veto"}`.
  Errors: 404 `Held run not found`; 409 `That run has already been settled`;
  400 — `This tool consults no oracle`, every `checkOverrides` sentence
  (`parameter "${name}" is not open to intervention`; `the roll is not open
  to intervention`; `a dice roll is set by its faces, not a number`; `this
  roll is not dice`; `this roll takes ${diceCount} dice, not ${n}`; `the
  ${field} is not open to intervention`; `there is no outcome row ${i}`;
  `effect ${i} is not open to intervention`), `CustomToolRunError`'s message,
  the Zod issue sentences joined `; `, and at the HTTP edge `The request body
  is not JSON`. HTTP: `GET /api/v1/chats/{id}/custom-tools?action=
  interventions`; `POST …?action=intervention-preview | intervention-reask |
  intervention-resolve` with bodies `{id, overrides}` / `{id, overrides}` /
  `{id, decision}`; an unknown action → the existing `Unknown action: <x>`
  envelope. Shapes (key order as v4's `view.ts` / `interventions.ts`; absent
  optionals OMITTED): `InterventionHoldView {id, tool, title, characterName:
  string|null, entrance: "llm"|"trigger", triggerPhrase?, createdAt: ms,
  eligible: {fields: string[], params: string[], effects: number[]},
  hasCharacter, definition: {parameters, outcomes: [{when, state, message,
  brief?}], effects, revealOdds, hasLlm, diceSides?}, baseline: Intervention
  Preview, consults: [{ok, output, prompt}]}`; `InterventionPreview {run:
  InterventionRunView, staleAnswer, promptNow?, clampedParams: string[],
  warnings: string[]}`; `InterventionRunView {params, rollForm, notation?,
  raw, diceRolls?, diceBreakdown, rollSpan: RunSpan, value, valueSpan: RunSpan,
  outcomeIndex, tableOutcomeIndex?, state, message, chipLabel?, title,
  visibility, announcer: "pascal"|"character", llm?: {ok, output, prompt},
  effects: InterventionEffectView[]}`; `InterventionEffectView {index, kind:
  "write"|"wardrobe"|"inventory"|"log", label, fired, skipped?, value?,
  items?, log?}`; `RunSpan {min, max, minExclusive?: true, maxExclusive?:
  true}`; `InterventionOverrides {params?, roll?, dice?: number[], value?,
  llmPrompt?, llmAnswer?: {ok, output}, outcomeIndex?, message?, state?,
  chipLabel?, title?, visibility?, announcer?, effects?: Record<"<index>",
  EffectPin>}`; `EffectPin {mode: "auto"|"on"|"off", value?, items?:
  string[], log?}`; the decision `{kind: "deal", overrides} | {kind:
  "as-rolled"} | {kind: "veto"}`. Event: `RealtimeTopic::CustomToolInterventions`
  = `"customToolInterventions"`, scoped by chat id, on hold add and on settle;
  SPA key `customToolsKeys.interventions(chatId) = ['customTools', chatId,
  'interventions']` (v5's prefix) + `customToolsKeys.all` in `ALL_REALTIME_
  PREFIXES`. `pascalMeta.intervention` (the LAST key of `pascalMeta`):
  `{resolvedAt: ISO, decision: "deal", changed: string[], dealt: {params, raw,
  diceRolls?, value, outcomeIndex, state, message, chipLabel?, toolTitle,
  visibility, announcer, llm?, effects?: [{index, fired, target, next?}]},
  reasks?, staleAnswer?: true}` — absent when never held or let stand as
  rolled; `PascalMetaIn.intervention: Option<serde_json::Value>` (opaque).
  The run result on every wire that spreads it (the chat POST `result`, the
  Workbench preview / `BenchRoll`): `…, llm?, chipLabel?, effects?, draw:
  {form:"range", unit} | {form:"dice", faces}, rollSpan, valueSpan,
  llmPromptRendered?, tableOutcomeIndex?`. The Workbench library entry
  `intervention: boolean` after `triggerCount` (before `effect`). The
  chat-settings key `customToolIntervention?: boolean` (GET / PUT) — P4.D341
  (row) + P4.D356 (PUT arm); the SPA Automation row posts
  `{customToolIntervention: value}`. The guard `pascal_intervention_wire_
  contract.rs` pins the verbs, variants, topic string and the three error
  sentences so P4.D346 authors blind. The definition grammar: `intervene?:
  boolean` on parameters and on effects; `intervention?: {enabled: boolean,
  fields?: {roll?, value?, llmPrompt?, llmAnswer?, outcome?, message?, state?,
  chipLabel?, title?, visibility?, announcer?}}` AFTER `announceAsCharacter`,
  BEFORE `parameters` (`KNOWN_TOP_LEVEL_KEYS` 18, both twins; `INTERVENTION_
  FIELD_KEYS` in that order; an empty block refused — `enabled` required);
  the ONE corpus regen + the byte-equal SPA copy.

- **C9 — the WCAG rules R1–R12 (P4.D358 writes; EVERY SPA lane applies to
  the files it owns and records a HANDOFF for the census):**
  - **R1 Modal dialog surface** (`dff76ebef`): the dialog CARD (never the
    overlay) carries `role="dialog"` — `role="alertdialog"` where the
    dialog-roles table says so — `aria-modal="true"`, `aria-labelledby=
    "<titleId>"` with `id="<titleId>"` on the visible title element (a
    per-instance id: `inject(UniqueId)` if the SPA has one, else a module
    counter `qt-dialog-<n>`); `tabindex="-1"`; the shared focus directive
    (`qtDialogFocus`, P4.D358's `ui/dialog-focus.ts`) for focus-in / Tab-trap
    / restore; Escape closes unless the host's busy gate says otherwise (keep
    v4's gate expression). For a `qt-modal` host: nothing — the primitive
    does it.
  - **R2 Non-modal floating surface** (`FloatingDialog`): `role="dialog"
    aria-modal="false" aria-labelledby`, `tabindex="-1"`, focus in on open
    and back to the opener on close, NO Tab trap, close button `aria-label=
    "Close"`, Escape inside closes it (topmost: `stopPropagation`), Escape
    elsewhere closes it only while `!document.querySelector('[aria-modal=
    "true"]')`, both skipping `defaultPrevented`.
  - **R3 Icon-only buttons** carry v4's `aria-label` byte: `Close`, `Cancel`,
    `Dismiss message`, `Close gallery`, `` Remove alias ${alias} ``, `Up one
    folder`, `Toggle model list` (+ `aria-expanded`).
  - **R4 Visible label ↔ control:** `<label for="<id>">` + `id="<id>"` on the
    control; never a bare `<span>` caption beside a control.
  - **R5 Heading-captioned control:** `aria-labelledby="<headingId>"` with
    `id` on the heading (ProfilesTab / ImageGenerationCard / ModelBehaviorCard
    / FieldSelectionStep / SuggestionCard / ContextCompression switch).
  - **R6 Placeholder-only / caption-less control:** `aria-label` with v4's
    string (never invent a string; where v4 has none, do not add one).
  - **R7 Switch:** `role="switch"` + `aria-checked` + a name (`aria-label` or
    `aria-labelledby`); the Prospero one is `type="button"` too.
  - **R8 Clickable non-button:** `role="button" tabindex="0" (keydown)=
    "activateOnKey($event)"` (P4.D358's `ui/activate-on-key.ts`) with
    `aria-expanded` (FileTable) / `aria-pressed` (gallery tile, quick-hide
    tags) where v4 sets them. Prefer a real `<button>` where the layout
    allows (v4 kept spans only inside `<tr>` / heading contexts and under
    the uppercase-button themes).
  - **R9 Live region for notices:** the toast container `role="status"
    aria-live="polite" data-toast-container`; an error toast `role="alert"`
    (P4.D358 only).
  - **R10 Colour-only state gets words:** `<span class="sr-only">Unmoderated
    chat.</span>`; ` (overheard)` appended to the whisper label.
  - **R11 Async result announced:** `<span class="sr-only" role="status"
    aria-live="polite">…</span>` (DocumentPane: `editingIndicator ??
    (saveLabel === 'Saved' ? 'Saved' : '')`).
  - **R12 Pointer targets ≥ 24 px** — CSS, P4.D357 (`.qt-tab-close`);
    templates: `min-w-6 min-h-6` on the inline copy-id button (P4.D358).

  OPTIMIZER` → 'Optimizer', `EXTERNAL_PROMPT` → 'Ext Prompt', `AUTO_CONFIGURE`
  → 'Auto-Config', `ANSWER_CONFIRMATION` → 'Confirm'; `||` → `??`; the
  `system` group gains `SCENE_STATE_TRACKING`, `image` the four image types);
  the union keeps both.
- **C16 — the SPA stand-in / gate rule:** every SPA lane that consumes a
  server contract not yet in its tree authors behind a named gate constant
  (`P4D341_LANDED`, `P4D343_SERVER_LANDED`, `P4D345_SERVER_LANDED`,
  `P4D347_SERVER_LANDED`, `P4D349_SERVER_LANDED` — as each order spells
  them; the `BRAHMA_CONSOLE_SERVER_LANDED` precedent at `e2e/settings-brahma-
  console-flow.spec.ts:30`) with throwaway stand-ins deleted at the flip;
  `core-contract.ts` additions are interfaces only, each in its own
  `// === P4.D3xx … ===` fence.

append-only for EVERY lane (the unifier union-merges them); `drift-ledger.md`
is `/unify`'s. "Stage" names the base: `main` at the orders' commit, or the
named stacking commit.
